Final cell mask: iscell AND NOT interneuron\n    # is_int indexes into cell_indices, so we need to map back\n    final_cell_indices = cell_indices[~is_int]\n    n_neurons = len(final_cell_indices)\n\n    if n_neurons == 0:\n        return None, None, None, 0, {}\n\n    # Get filtered deconvolved data: (n_frames, n_neurons)\n    deconv_filtered = deconvolved[:, final_cell_indices]\n\n    # --- Process trials ---\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n\n    # Parse scene for reward zone info\n    env_before, zone_before, env_after, zone_after = parse_scene(scene)\n\n    dt = 1.0 / frame_rate  # time per frame in seconds\n\n    prev_rewarded = 0  # For the first trial, no previous trial → 0\n\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        t_start = trial_start_idx[t]\n        t_end = teleport_idx[t]\n\n        if t_end <= t_start:\n            continue\n\n        # --- Neural data ---\n        # (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n        neural = deconv_filtered[t_start:t_end, :].T.astype(np.float32)\n\n        n_tp = neural.shape[1]\n        if n_tp < 2:\n            continue\n\n        # --- Position and behavior ---\n        pos_trial = position[t_start:t_end]\n        speed_trial = speed[t_start:t_end]\n        lick_trial = lick[t_start:t_end]\n\n        # Clamp position to [0, 450]\n        pos_trial = np.clip(pos_trial, 0, 450)\n\n        # --- Determine active reward zone ---\n        zone_letter = get_reward_zone_for_trial(scene, t)\n        zone_start, zone_end = REWARD_ZONES[zone_letter]\n        zone_idx = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}[zone_letter]\n\n        # --- Determine environment ---\n        env = get_env_for_trial(scene, t)\n        env_binary = 0 if env == 1 else 1  # ENV1=0, ENV2=1\n\n        # --- Determine reward outcome ---\n        t_start_time = timestamps[t_start]\n        t_end_time = timestamps[t_end - 1]\n        rewarded = int(np.any((reward_ts >= t_start_time) & (reward_ts <= t_end_time)))\n\n        # --- Compute outputs ---\n        # 1. Distance to reward zone (time-varying, discretized)\n        dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(pos_trial, zone_start, zone_end)\n        dist_disc = discretize_distance(dist)\n\n        # 2. Absolute position (time-varying, discretized)\n        pos_disc = discretize_position(pos_trial)\n\n        # 3. Speed (time-varying, discretized)\n        speed_disc = discretize_speed(np.abs(speed_trial))\n\n        # 4. Lick (time-varying, binary)\n        lick_binary = (lick_trial > 0).astype(np.int64)\n\n        # 5. Reward zone location (per-trial)\n        # 6. Reward outcome (per-trial)\n\n        # Stack time-varying outputs: (n_output, n_timepoints)\n        # Time-varying: dist, pos, speed, lick (4 vars, n_timepoints each)\n        # Per-trial: reward_zone, reward_outcome (2 vars, scalar each)\n        # For per-trial outputs, broadcast to length 1\n        output = np.stack([\n            dist_disc,\n            pos_disc,\n            speed_disc,\n            lick_binary,\n            np.full(n_tp, zone_idx, dtype=np.int64),\n            np.full(n_tp, rewarded, dtype=np.int64),\n        ], axis=0)  # (6, n_timepoints)\n\n        # --- Compute inputs ---\n        # 1. Time from start of trial (seconds, continuous, time-varying)\n        time_from_start = np.arange(n_tp, dtype=np.float32) * dt\n\n        # 2. Environment type (binary, per-trial)\n        # 3. Trial number (continuous, per-trial)\n        # 4. Previous trial outcome (binary, per-trial)\n\n        input_data = np.stack([\n            time_from_start,\n            np.full(n_tp, env_binary, dtype=np.float32),\n            np.full(n_tp, t, dtype=np.float32),\n            np.full(n_tp, prev_rewarded, dtype=np.float32),\n        ], axis=0)  # (4, n_timepoints)\n\n        neural_trials.append(neural)\n        input_trials.append(input_data)\n        output_trials.append(output)\n\n        # Update previous trial outcome for next iteration\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n\ni. Several cases are handled:\n- **Neural/behavior length mismatch**: If the number of neural time bins differs from behavior timestamps, data is cropped to the minimum of the two with a warning printed.\n- **Short trials**: Trials with fewer than 50 timepoints (`min_ntimepoints`) are skipped with a warning.\n- **Missing reward zone data**: Trials where `reward_zone` is never active (all zeros) get NaN positions, and the Viterbi algorithm handles these via uniform emission (assignment driven by neighboring trials' transitions).\n- **Reward timestamp alignment**: An assertion verifies reward times align within half a time bin of behavior timestamps.\n\nii.\n```python\nif T != len(timestamps):\n    print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n    T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n    spike_data = spike_data[:T]\n    timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n...\nif idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n    print(f\"Skipping trial {trial}...\")\n    istrial[trial] = False\n    continue\n```\n\niii. Set fluorescence outside trials to NaN\n   339\t    2. Neuropil subtraction: F -= 0.7 * Fneu  (single-channel default)\n   340\t    3. Add back neuropil mean per trial (to avoid dividing by small numbers)\n   341\t    4. Maximin baseline: smooth -> minimum filter (300 samples) -> maximum filter (300 samples)\n   342\t    5. dF/F = (F_corrected - baseline) / |baseline|\n   343\t    6. Smooth with 2-sample Gaussian per trial\n   344\t\n   345\t    Args:\n   346\t        F: (n_timepoints, n_cells) raw fluorescence\n   347\t        Fneu: (n_timepoints, n_cells) neuropil fluorescence\n   348\t        trial_starts: list of trial start frame indices\n   349\t        trial_ends: list of trial end frame indices\n   350\t        frame_rate: imaging frame rate\n   351\t\n   352\t    Returns:\n   353\t        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) dF/F traces (NaN outside trials)\n   354\t    \"\"\"\n   355\t    n_frames, n_cells = F.shape\n   356\t    neu_coef = 0.7\n   357\t\n   358\t    # Transpose to (n_cells, n_timepoints) for processing\n   359\t    F_t = F.T.astype(np.float64).copy()\n   360\t    Fneu_t = Fneu.T.astype(np.float64).copy()\n   361\t\n   362\t    # Initialize with NaN (outside trials)\n   363\t    f_ = np.full_like(F_t, np.nan)\n   364\t    fneu_ = np.full_like(Fneu_t, np.nan)\n   365\t\n   366\t    # Copy only trial data\n   367\t    for start, stop in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   368\t        f_[:, start:stop] = F_t[:, start:stop]\n   369\t        fneu_[:, start:stop] = Fneu_t[:, start:stop]\n   370\t\n   371\t    # Neuropil subtraction (single channel default in reference code)\n   372\t    f_ -= neu_coef * fneu_\n   373\t\n   374\t    # Baseline computation per trial\n   375\t    baseline = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n   376\t    dff = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n   377\t\n   378\t    window = 300  # ~20 seconds at 15.5 Hz\n   379\t\n   380\t    for start, stop in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   381\t        trial_len = stop - start\n   382\t        if trial_len < 3:\n   383\t            continue\n   384\t\n   385\t        # Add back neuropil mean per trial (reference code line 434)\n   386\t        f_[:, start:stop] += neu_coef * np.nanmean(\n   387\t            fneu_[:, start:stop], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   388\t\n   389\t        # Smooth with Gaussian sigma=15 (reference code line 440: nansmooth with [0, 15])\n   390\t        bl = np.copy(f_[:, start:stop])\n   391\t        for c in range(n_cells):\n   392\t            bl[c] = ndi.gaussian_filter1d(bl[c], sigma=15)\n   393\t\n   394\t        # Minimum filter
